async function schoolReadPost(action,payload={}){
  const url=await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();
  const response=await net.fetch(url,{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json;charset=UTF-8','accept':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({action,...payload})});
  let data={};try{data=await response.json();}catch{}
  if(!response.ok)throw schoolReadError(data?.message||('School Read API HTTP '+response.status),data?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_HTTP_ERROR');
  return data;
}
async function loginSchoolReadUser({name='',email=''}={}){
  const deviceId=await getOrCreateSchoolReadDeviceId();
  const result=await schoolReadPost('login',{name:String(name||'').trim(),email:String(email||'').trim(),deviceId});
  if(result?.ok&&result?.token){await saveSchoolReadSession({token:String(result.token),deviceId,user:result.user||{},expiresAt:Date.now()+Math.max(300,Number(result.expiresIn||0))*1000,savedAt:new Date().toISOString()});liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;}
  return result;
}
async function schoolReadSessionStatus({verify=true}={}){
  let endpointReady=true,endpointError='';
  try{await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();}catch(error){endpointReady=false;endpointError=String(error?.message||error);}
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)return {ok:endpointReady,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:endpointError,user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  if(!verify)return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady,user:session.user||null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  try{const result=await schoolReadPost('verify-token',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId});if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'UEP 세션 확인 실패',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_TOKEN_INVALID');session.user=result.user||session.user||{};await saveSchoolReadSession(session);return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady:true,user:session.user,expiresAt:result.expiresAt||'',mode:'school_read_api'};}catch(error){await clearSchoolReadSession();return {ok:false,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:String(error?.message||error),code:error?.code||'',user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};}
}
async function schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges){
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)throw schoolReadError('UEP 로그인이 필요합니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_LOGIN_REQUIRED');
  const result=await schoolReadPost('batch-read',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId,spreadsheetId,ranges});
  if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'학교 데이터를 읽지 못했습니다.',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_BATCH_FAILED');
  return result.valueRanges||[];
}

function createWindow() {
  const win = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1500, height: 960, minWidth: 1050, minHeight: 700,
    backgroundColor: "#f4f8fd", title: "UNHO Education Platform (UEP)", frame: false,
    icon: path.join(__dirname, "..", "assets", "unho-uep-icon.png"),
    webPreferences: { preload: path.join(__dirname, "preload.cjs"), contextIsolation: true, nodeIntegration: false },
  });
  mainWindow = win;
  win.setMenuBarVisibility(false);
  win.on("close", () => {
    // A hidden Comcigan BrowserWindow used to keep Electron alive after the main
    // UI disappeared. Destroy auxiliary windows before the last visible window closes.
    destroyAuxiliaryWindows("main-window-close");
  });
  win.on("closed", () => {
    mainWindow = null;
    beginCleanShutdown("main-window-closed");
  });
  win.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, "..", "index.html"));
}

async function migrateStableUserFile(fileName) {
  const target=path.join(stableUserDataRoot(),fileName), legacy=path.join(legacyUserDataRoot(),fileName);
  try { await fs.access(target); return; } catch {}
  try { await fs.mkdir(stableUserDataRoot(),{recursive:true}); await fs.copyFile(legacy,target); } catch {}
}
async function migrateStableUserData() {
  // Local State에는 Windows safeStorage가 사용하는 Chromium OS encryption key 정보가 들어 있습니다.
  // 기존 버전의 userData에서 최초 1회 같이 옮겨야 기존 암호문을 복호화할 가능성이 유지됩니다.
  for (const fileName of ["Local State","school-board-state.json","quick-launch-settings.json","google-service-account.bin","google-service-account.recovery.json","google-user-oauth.bin","google-user-oauth.recovery.json","neis-api-key.bin","neis-dashboard-cache.json","comcigan-timetable-cache.json"]) await migrateStableUserFile(fileName);
}
let comciganWindow = null;

let shutdownStarted = false;

async function appendShutdownLog(message) {
  try {
    const file = path.join(stableUserDataRoot(), "shutdown-diagnostics.log");
    await fs.mkdir(stableUserDataRoot(), { recursive: true });
    await fs.appendFile(file, `${new Date().toISOString()} ${message}\n`, "utf8");
  } catch {}
}
function destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason="shutdown") {
  try { appendShutdownLog(`destroy-aux:${reason}:comcigan=${Boolean(comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed())}`); } catch {}
  try {
    if (comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed()) comciganWindow.destroy();
  } catch {}
  comciganWindow = null;
}
function beginCleanShutdown(reason="user-close") {
  if (shutdownStarted) return;
  shutdownStarted = true;
  try { appendShutdownLog(`shutdown-start:${reason}:pid=${process.pid}:windows=${BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length}`); } catch {}
  destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason);
  // Give Electron a short grace period to release renderer/GPU resources. If the
  // normal quit sequence stalls, force only THIS Electron process to exit.
  setTimeout(() => {
    try { appendShutdownLog(`shutdown-force-exit:${reason}:windows=${BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length}`); } catch {}
    try { app.exit(0); } catch {}
  }, 1500).unref?.();
}

function compactDateKey(date) {
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}

async function saveDormOuting(payload={}){
  const allowedRoles=new Set(['admin','grade_head','grade_manager','homeroom']);
  if(payload.role&&!allowedRoles.has(String(payload.role)))throw new Error('학사외출 교사등록 권한이 없습니다.');
  const date=String(payload.date||'').slice(0,10),category=String(payload.category||'외출'),studentNo=String(payload.studentNo||'').replace(/\D/g,''),name=String(payload.name||'').trim();
  if(!date||!studentNo||!name)throw new Error('학생·학번·날짜 정보를 확인해 주세요.');
  const home=/퇴소|귀가/.test(category),outTime=String(payload.outTime||''),returnTime=String(payload.returnTime||'');
  if(!outTime)throw new Error('외출·귀가 시간을 입력해 주세요.');
  if(!home&&!returnTime)throw new Error('복귀 예정시간을 입력해 주세요.');
  const account=await resolveSchoolServiceAccount();if(!validateServiceAccount(account))throw new Error('학교 공용 Google 쓰기 연결을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account),now=new Date().toISOString(),id=String(payload.id||`OUT-TEACHER-${crypto.randomUUID()}`),grade=String(payload.grade||studentNo.slice(0,1)||'1'),className=String(payload.className||studentNo.slice(1,2)||''),number=String(payload.number||studentNo.slice(-2)).replace(/^0/,'');
  const existing=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'02_학사외출_일자별'!A3:AD5000"]),matrix=existing?.[0]?.values||[],headers=(matrix[0]||[]).map(x=>String(x||'').trim()),col=(...names)=>names.map(n=>headers.indexOf(n)).find(i=>i>=0);
  const dcol=col('외출일자','신청일자','운영일','일자','날짜'),ncol=col('학번','학생학번'),idcol=col('학생ID','학생 ID'),ccol=col('외출구분','신청구분','구분'),tcol=col('외출시간','출발예정시간','출발시간');
  const duplicate=matrix.slice(1).some(r=>String(r[dcol]||'').slice(0,10)===date&&((ncol>=0&&String(r[ncol]||'').replace(/\.0$/,'')===studentNo)||(idcol>=0&&String(r[idcol]||'')===String(payload.studentId||'')))&&String(r[ccol]||'')===category&&String(r[tcol]||'')===outTime);
  if(duplicate)throw new Error('같은 학생의 동일한 학사외출 기록이 이미 있습니다.');
  const row=[[id,'',date,String(payload.studentId||''),grade,className,number,name,'교사등록',category,home?'귀가형':'복귀형',outTime,home?'':returnTime,home?returnTime:'',String(payload.reason||''),String(payload.destination||''),String(payload.guardian||''),String(payload.note||''),String(payload.status||'확인'),now,now,'UEP반영',now,String(payload.writer||''),'','','Y','교사 직접등록','담임·관리자 직접 확인',now]];
  await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'02_학사외출_일자별'!A:AD",row);
  const record={id,studentId:String(payload.studentId||''),studentNo,name,date,className,number,category,outTime,returnTime,reason:String(payload.reason||''),destination:String(payload.destination||''),note:String(payload.note||''),confirmed:String(payload.writer||''),status:'확인'};if(liveDataCache){liveDataCache={...liveDataCache,dormOutings:[...(liveDataCache.dormOutings||[]).filter(x=>String(x.id||'')!==String(id)),record],syncedAt:new Date().toISOString()};liveDataFetchedAt=Date.now();}return {ok:true,id,record};

function schoolConnectionLegacyRoots(){
  const appData=process.env.APPDATA||app.getPath("appData");
  return [...new Set([
    stableUserDataRoot(),legacyUserDataRoot(),
    path.join(appData,"UNHO Education Platform"),
    path.join(appData,"unho-education-platform"),
    path.join(appData,"UEP"),
    path.join(appData,"UEP School Board"),
    path.join(appData,"School Board")
  ].filter(Boolean).map(value=>path.resolve(value)))];
}
async function readCredentialRecoveryFrom(filePath){
  const payload=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(filePath,"utf8"));
  if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=="aes-256-gcm")throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm",recoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}
async function resolveSchoolServiceAccount(){
  try{const current=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(validateServiceAccount(current))return current;}catch{}
  for(const root of schoolConnectionLegacyRoots()){
    for(const fileName of ["google-service-account.recovery.json","google-service-account.json"]){
      const candidate=path.join(root,fileName);
      try{
        const recovered=fileName.endsWith("recovery.json")?await readCredentialRecoveryFrom(candidate):JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(candidate,"utf8"));
        if(!validateServiceAccount(recovered))continue;
        await saveEncrypted(credentialPath(),recovered);
        console.log(`[UEP] 학교 공용 Google 연결을 기존 UEP 저장소에서 복구했습니다: ${path.basename(root)}`);
        return recovered;
      }catch{}
    }
  }
  const error=new Error("이 PC에서 기존 학교 공용 Google 연결을 찾지 못했습니다. 관리자 배포본의 공용 연결 복구 상태를 확인해 주세요.");
  error.code="UEP_SCHOOL_CONNECTION_NOT_PROVISIONED";throw error;
}

async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function normalizeSheetHeader(value){return String(value??"").replace(/\r?\n/g," ").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();}
function findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,required){
  const wanted=required.map(x=>String(x));
  const limit=Math.min(Array.isArray(matrix)?matrix.length:0,12);
  for(let i=0;i<limit;i+=1){
    const row=(matrix[i]||[]).map(normalizeSheetHeader);
    if(wanted.every(name=>row.includes(name))) return {index:i,headers:row};
  }
  return null;
}
async function saveStudentContact(payload={}) {
  const allowed={phone:["학생연락처"],email:["학생이메일","이메일","이메일주소"],guardian1Phone:["보호자1연락처"],guardian2Phone:["보호자2연락처"]};
  const field=String(payload.field||"");
  if(!allowed[field]) throw new Error("수정할 수 없는 학생정보 항목입니다.");
  const role=String(payload.role||"");
  if(!["admin","grade_head","homeroom"].includes(role)) throw new Error("학생 연락정보 수정 권한이 없습니다."); /* UEP_08283_CONTACT_WRITE_SCOPE */
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'04_학생연락식별정보'!A1:Z1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const header=findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["학생ID"]);
  if(!header) throw new Error("04_학생연락식별정보의 헤더에서 학생ID를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const studentId=String(payload.studentId||"").trim();
  if(!studentId) throw new Error("학생ID가 없어 저장할 수 없습니다.");
  const idCol=header.headers.indexOf("학생ID");
  let fieldCol=-1;
  for(const name of allowed[field]){ const idx=header.headers.indexOf(name); if(idx>=0){fieldCol=idx;break;} }
  if(fieldCol<0) throw new Error(`04_학생연락식별정보에 ${allowed[field][0]} 열이 없습니다.`);
  let dataRow=-1;
  for(let r=header.index+1;r<matrix.length;r+=1){ if(String(matrix[r]?.[idCol]||"").trim()===studentId){dataRow=r;break;} }
  const value=String(payload.value??"").trim();
  if(dataRow<0){
    const row=Array(Math.max(header.headers.length,fieldCol+1)).fill("");
    row[idCol]=studentId; row[fieldCol]=value;
    const useCol=header.headers.indexOf("현재사용여부"); if(useCol>=0)row[useCol]="Y";
    await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'04_학생연락식별정보'!A:Z",[row]);
  }else{
    const sheetRow=dataRow+1;
    const col=columnLetter(fieldCol);
    await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'04_학생연락식별정보'!${col}${sheetRow}:${col}${sheetRow}`,[[value]]);
  }
  if(liveDataCache?.students){liveDataCache={...liveDataCache,students:liveDataCache.students.map(student=>String(student.id||'')===studentId?{...student,[field]:value}:student)};liveDataFetchedAt=Date.now();}
  return {ok:true,contactPatch:{studentId,field,value}};
}

async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
  let sheetRow=-1;
  if(id){
    const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
    if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
  }else{
    let max=0;
    rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
    id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
  }
  const values=officialAttendanceRow(payload,id);
  if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
  const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
  return {ok:true,id,patch:operational.patch||{}};
}

async function deleteOfficialAttendance(id="") {
  const key=String(id||"").trim();
  if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000"]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 공결 기록을 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const sheetRow=pos+4;
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`);
  const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
  return {ok:true,patch:operational.patch||{}};
}

function dormRuleDateValue(value){
  if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return '';
  const raw=String(value).trim();
  if(/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)){
    const serial=Number(raw);
    if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000){
      const d=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+Math.round(serial*86400000));
      if(!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())){
        const y=d.getUTCFullYear(),mo=String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),day=String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0');
        const hh=String(d.getUTCHours()).padStart(2,'0'),mm=String(d.getUTCMinutes()).padStart(2,'0');
        return `${y}-${mo}-${day}T${hh}:${mm}`;
      }
    }
  }
  const mm=raw.replace(' ','T').match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})T(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return mm?`${mm[1]}-${String(mm[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(mm[3]).padStart(2,'0')}T${String(mm[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${mm[5]}`:raw;
}

    effect:String(row[6]||'').trim(),entryTime:String(row[7]||'').trim(),exitTime:String(row[8]||'').trim(),

    note:String(row[14]||'').trim(),modifiedAt:String(row[15]||'').trim(),writer:String(row[16]||'').trim(),source:String(row[17]||'').trim(),

async function saveDormRule(payload={}){
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), now=new Date().toISOString();
  const id=String(payload.id||`DORM-RULE-${crypto.randomUUID()}`).trim();
  const range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
  const row=dormRuleRow(payload,id,now);
  if(pos>=0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`,row);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'40_학사외출규칙'!A:X",row);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  const record={...payload,id,priority:Number(payload.priority||dormRulePriority(payload.category)),active:payload.active!==false,modifiedAt:now,writer:String(payload.writer||"UEP"),source:String(payload.source||"UEP 규칙센터")};
  return {ok:true,id,record};
}

async function deleteDormRule(id=""){
  const key=String(id||"").trim(); if(!key) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙 ID가 없습니다.");
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account), range="'40_학사외출규칙'!A3:X1000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const rows=(result?.[0]?.values||[]).slice(1);
  const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===key);
  if(pos<0) throw new Error("삭제할 학사외출 규칙을 시트에서 찾지 못했습니다.");
  await clearSheetValues(token,UEP_RULES_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'40_학사외출규칙'!A${pos+4}:X${pos+4}`);
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
  return {ok:true,id:key};
}

  const existing=dataRow>=0?(matrix[dataRow]||[]):[];
  const obj={};headers.forEach((h,i)=>{if(h)obj[h]=existing[i]??'';});
  const id=requestedId||('ACP-'+crypto.randomUUID());
  const action=String(payload.action||'save');
  obj['포인트ID']=id;obj['대학ID']=String(payload.universityId??obj['대학ID']??'').trim();obj['대학명']=String(payload.universityName??obj['대학명']??'').trim();obj['캠퍼스']=String(payload.campus??obj['캠퍼스']??'').trim();obj['기준학년도']=String(payload.year??obj['기준학년도']??'2028').trim();obj['전형명']=String(payload.track??obj['전형명']??'').trim();obj['모집단위']=String(payload.unit??obj['모집단위']??'').trim();obj['포인트구분']=String(payload.kind??obj['포인트구분']??'학년부추가').trim()||'학년부추가';
  if(action!=='delete'){const text=String(payload.text??'').trim();if(!text)throw new Error('상담포인트 내용을 입력해 주세요.');obj['상담포인트']=text;}

function inputCenterNatureToStoredType(nature=""){
  const n=String(nature||"");
  if(n==="보고서")return "보고서·탐구보고서";
  if(n==="신청")return "프로그램 신청서";
  if(n==="만족도조사")return "만족도·설문조사";
  if(n==="출결·명단")return "출결·참여명단";
  return n||"기타";
}

function inputCenterSemester(value="",submittedAt=""){
  const t=String(value||""); if(/(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)|1학기/.test(t))return "1학기"; if(/(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)|2학기/.test(t))return "2학기";
  const m=String(submittedAt||"").match(/\d{4}[-/.](\d{1,2})/);return m&&Number(m[1])<=7?"1학기":"2학기";
}

  try {
    const account = await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
    if (!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");

    const matrix = await readNoticeSheetMatrix(token, "01_사용자계정", "A1:Z500");
    const header = findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["사용자ID","이메일","성명","역할","사용여부"]);
    if(!header) throw new Error("01_사용자계정 헤더를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    const headers=header.headers, idx=name=>headers.indexOf(name), id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
    const rowIndex=matrix.findIndex((row,i)=>i>header.index&&String(row?.[idx("사용자ID")]||"").trim()===id);
    if(rowIndex<0) throw new Error("수정할 사용자를 찾지 못했습니다.");
    const updates={
      "역할":String(payload.role??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("역할")]??"").trim(),
      "관리자여부":payload.admin!=null?(payload.admin?"Y":"N"):String(matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("관리자여부")]||"N"),
      "담당학년":String(payload.grade??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("담당학년")]??"1").trim(),
      "담당반":String(payload.classNo??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("담당반")]??"").trim(),
      "조회범위":String(payload.viewScope??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("조회범위")]??"").trim(),
      "수정범위":String(payload.editScope??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("수정범위")]??"").trim(),
      "사용여부":payload.active!=null?(payload.active?"Y":"N"):String(matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("사용여부")]||"Y")
    };
    const row=Array.from({length:headers.length},(_,i)=>matrix[rowIndex]?.[i]??"");
    for (const [name,value] of Object.entries(updates)) { const col=idx(name); if(col>=0) row[col]=value; }
    await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'01_사용자계정'!A${rowIndex+1}:${columnLetter(headers.length-1)}${rowIndex+1}`,[row]);
    liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
    return {ok:true,id};
  } catch(error) { return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||"사용자 권한 저장 실패"}; }

async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

async function fetchOperationalData({credentials=null}={}){

function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const key = recoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const payload = {
    version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
    salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
  };
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
}

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
}

async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

}

  try {
    const credentials = await resolveSchoolServiceAccount();
    if (validateServiceAccount(credentials)) return {ok:true,encryption:safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable(),local:false,mode:"service_account",account:credentials.client_email||"",userOAuth:false,autoConnected:true,loginIdentityGateway:true,approvalRequired:false,tokenExchangeRequired:false};
  } catch (error) {}
  return {ok:true,local:true,mode:"login_identity",account:"",userOAuth:false,autoConnected:true,loginIdentityGateway:true,approvalRequired:false,tokenExchangeRequired:false,reason:"UEP 로그인 이름·이메일로 교사·담임을 식별합니다."};
});
ipcMain.handle("google:authorizeUser", async () => {
  return {ok:true,skipped:true,mode:"login_identity",autoConnected:true,approvalRequired:false,tokenExchangeRequired:false,message:"별도 Google 계정 승인은 사용하지 않습니다. UEP 로그인 이름·이메일로 담임 권한을 적용합니다."};
});
ipcMain.handle("google:diagnoseUserOAuth", async () => {
  const result={authorized:false,account:"",tokenOk:false,sheetsOk:false,reason:"",status:0};
  try {
    const saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
    if(!saved){result.reason="Google 계정 연결 정보가 없습니다.";return result;}
    result.authorized=true;result.account=String(saved.email||"");
    const token=await getGoogleUserSheetsToken();
    result.tokenOk=Boolean(token);
    try {
      await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'01_사용자계정'!A1:B2"]);
      result.sheetsOk=true;
    } catch(error) {
      result.reason=String(error?.message||error||"Google 시트 조회 실패");
      const match=result.reason.match(/\((\d{3})\)/);result.status=match?Number(match[1]):0;
    }
    return result;
  } catch(error) {result.reason=String(error?.message||error||"Google OAuth 진단 실패");return result;}
});ipcMain.handle("google:disconnectUser", async () => {
  try{return await disconnectGoogleUser();}catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||String(error)};}
});

      properties: ["openFile"],

    });
    if (selected.canceled || !selected.filePaths[0]) return { ok: false, canceled: true };

async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
  for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
    try{
      const encrypted=await fs.readFile(target);
      if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
        try{const value=JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));if(value&&typeof value==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(value);return value;}}catch{}
      }
      try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
    }catch{}
  }
  try{
    const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
    if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(recovered);return recovered;}
  }catch{}
  return null;
}
async function googleUserInfo(accessToken){
  const response=await net.fetch('https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo',{headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${accessToken}`}});
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 사용자 확인 실패 (${response.status})`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_USERINFO_FAILED');
  return await response.json();
}
async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}
async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,

    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,

    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });

  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function appendShutdownLog(message) {
  try {
    const file = path.join(stableUserDataRoot(), "shutdown-diagnostics.log");
    await fs.mkdir(stableUserDataRoot(), { recursive: true });
    await fs.appendFile(file, `${new Date().toISOString()} ${message}\n`, "utf8");
  } catch {}
}
function destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason="shutdown") {
  try { appendShutdownLog(`destroy-aux:${reason}:comcigan=${Boolean(comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed())}`); } catch {}
  try {
    if (comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed()) comciganWindow.destroy();
  } catch {}
  comciganWindow = null;
}
function beginCleanShutdown(reason="user-close") {
  if (shutdownStarted) return;
  shutdownStarted = true;
  try { appendShutdownLog(`shutdown-start:${reason}:pid=${process.pid}:windows=${BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length}`); } catch {}
  destroyAuxiliaryWindows(reason);
  // Give Electron a short grace period to release renderer/GPU resources. If the
  // normal quit sequence stalls, force only THIS Electron process to exit.
  setTimeout(() => {
    try { appendShutdownLog(`shutdown-force-exit:${reason}:windows=${BrowserWindow.getAllWindows().length}`); } catch {}
    try { app.exit(0); } catch {}
  }, 1500).unref?.();
}

function compactDateKey(date) {
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
}
function parseComciganPeriodStart(label="") {
  const text=String(label||"");
  let m=text.match(/(\d{2})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})\s*[~～-]/);
  if(m) return new Date(2000+Number(m[1]),Number(m[2])-1,Number(m[3]),12,0,0);
  m=text.match(/(?:\(|^)(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\s*[~～-]/);
  if(m){const now=new Date();return new Date(now.getFullYear(),Number(m[1])-1,Number(m[2]),12,0,0);}
  return null;
}

function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const key = recoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const payload = {
    version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
    salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
  };
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
}

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
}

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);

// __UEP_GOOGLE_MISSING_CREDENTIAL_08069__
async function readEncrypted(filePath) {
  let encrypted = null;
  try { encrypted = await fs.readFile(filePath); } catch (readError) {
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
      try { return await readCredentialRecovery(); } catch {}
    }
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath()) && (readError?.code === "ENOENT" || /no such file/i.test(String(readError?.message||"")))) {
      const friendly = new Error("담임 배포 PC의 Google 시트 연결 승인이 아직 준비되지 않았습니다.");
      friendly.code = "UEP_GOOGLE_CREDENTIAL_MISSING";
      friendly.setupRequired = true;
      throw friendly;
    }
    throw readError;
  }
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    try { return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted)); } catch {}
  }
  // 일부 구버전/수동 이관 파일이 평문 JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.
  try {
    const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
    if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, legacy); return legacy; }
  } catch {}
  // Google 서비스 계정은 safeStorage가 깨져도 동일 PC/사용자용 AES-GCM 복구본으로 자동 복원합니다.
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
    try {
      const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
      if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
        try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
        return recovered;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
  try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
  const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
  error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
  throw error;
}

  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");

}

// __UEP_GOOGLE_MISSING_CREDENTIAL_08069__
async function readEncrypted(filePath) {
  let encrypted = null;
  try { encrypted = await fs.readFile(filePath); } catch (readError) {
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
      try { return await readCredentialRecovery(); } catch {}
    }
    if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath()) && (readError?.code === "ENOENT" || /no such file/i.test(String(readError?.message||"")))) {
      const friendly = new Error("담임 배포 PC의 Google 시트 연결 승인이 아직 준비되지 않았습니다.");
      friendly.code = "UEP_GOOGLE_CREDENTIAL_MISSING";
      friendly.setupRequired = true;
      throw friendly;
    }
    throw readError;
  }
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    try { return JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted)); } catch {}
  }
  // 일부 구버전/수동 이관 파일이 평문 JSON인 경우 현재 형식으로 재저장합니다.
  try {
    const legacy = JSON.parse(encrypted.toString("utf8"));
    if (legacy && typeof legacy === "object") { await saveEncrypted(filePath, legacy); return legacy; }
  } catch {}
  // Google 서비스 계정은 safeStorage가 깨져도 동일 PC/사용자용 AES-GCM 복구본으로 자동 복원합니다.
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) {
    try {
      const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
      if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
        try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
        return recovered;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
  try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
  const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
  error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
  throw error;
}

async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}

  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 사용자 확인 실패 (${response.status})`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_USERINFO_FAILED');
  return await response.json();
}
async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}

async function ensureComciganWindow(schoolCode){
  if(comciganWindow && !comciganWindow.isDestroyed()) return comciganWindow;
  comciganWindow=new BrowserWindow({show:false,width:1280,height:900,webPreferences:{contextIsolation:true,sandbox:true}});
  comciganWindow.on("closed",()=>{comciganWindow=null;});
  await comciganWindow.loadURL(`https://timetable.s2h9.dev/?sc=${encodeURIComponent(schoolCode)}`);
  await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,1800));
  return comciganWindow;
}
async function fetchComciganRaw(schoolCode,period=1){
  const win=await ensureComciganWindow(schoolCode);
  const script=`(async()=>{
  const el=document.getElementById('__next_data__');
  let token=''; try{token=JSON.parse(el?.textContent||'{}').csrfToken||'';}catch{}
  if(!token) throw new Error('컴시간 CSRF 토큰을 찾지 못했습니다.');
  const url='/api/getalltimetable?schoolCode=${String(schoolCode).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')}&period=${Number(period)||1}&format=json';
  const r=await fetch(url,{credentials:'include',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json','x-csrf-token':token}});
  if(!r.ok) throw new Error('컴시간 HTTP '+r.status);
  return await r.json();
})()`;
  return await win.webContents.executeJavaScript(script,true);
}
function normalizeComciganTimetable(raw,targetDate,grade,classNo){
  const g=String(Number(grade)||1), c=String(Number(classNo)||1);
  const timetable=raw?.timetable||{};
  const gradeData=timetable[g]||timetable[Number(g)]||{};
  const schedule=gradeData[c]||gradeData[Number(c)]||[];
  const periods=Array.isArray(raw?.periods)?raw.periods:[];
  const periodId=selectComciganPeriod(periods,targetDate);
  const periodRow=periods.find(x=>Number(Array.isArray(x)?x[0]:x?.id)===periodId);
  const start=parseComciganPeriodStart(Array.isArray(periodRow)?periodRow[1]:periodRow?.label||"") || (()=>{const d=new Date(`${targetDate}T12:00:00`);const day=d.getDay();d.setDate(d.getDate()+(day===0?-6:1-day));return d;})();
  const weekdays=["월","화","수","목","금"];
  const days=weekdays.map((weekday,index)=>{
    const date=new Date(start); date.setDate(date.getDate()+index);
    const cells=Array.isArray(schedule[index])?schedule[index]:[];
    const lessons=cells.filter(Boolean).map(cell=>({
      period:Number(cell.classTime||cell.period||cell.classTimeNum||0),
      subject:String(cell.subject||cell.subjectName||""),
      teacher:String(cell.teacher||cell.teacherName||""),
      location:String(cell.location||cell.room||cell.classroom||""),
      grade:g,classNo:c,
      code:cell.code??null,
      isChanged:Boolean(cell.isChanged),changeInfo:cell.changeInfo||null,
    })).filter(x=>x.period>0);
    return {weekday,date:compactDateKey(date),lessons,rawLessons:lessons,rawRowCount:lessons.length,gradeWideRowCount:lessons.length,gradeMatchedRowCount:lessons.length,gradeClassValues:[c],source:"COMCIGAN"};
  });
  const teachers=raw?.teachers||{};
  const teacherTimetable=raw?.teacherTimetable||{};
  return {days,diagnostics:days.map(day=>({date:day.date,rowCount:day.lessons.length,complete:day.lessons.some(x=>x.period>=6)||day.weekday==="수"})),periods,classTime:raw?.classTime||[],teachers,teacherTimetable,schoolInfo:raw?.schoolInfo||null,lastUpdate:raw?.lastUpdate||"",source:"컴시간 웹",periodId};
}

function parseTimeRange(text) {
  const m=String(text||"").match(/(?:^|\s)([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})\s*(?:[-~～]|부터)\s*([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})/);
  if(m) return [`${m[1].padStart(2,"0")}:${m[2]}`,`${m[3].padStart(2,"0")}:${m[4]}`];
  const one=String(text||"").match(/(?:^|\s)([01]?\d|2[0-3])[:：](\d{2})/);
  return one ? [`${one[1].padStart(2,"0")}:${one[2]}`,""] : ["",""];
}
function classifyCalendarStatus(text) {
  const value=String(text||"");
  if(/취소|폐지/.test(value)) return "취소";
  if(/확정/.test(value)) return "확정";
  if(/예약|대관|사용/.test(value)) return "예약";
  if(/예정|계획|공고|제출기간/.test(value)) return "예정";
  return "상태 미기재";
}
function parseOwner(text) {
  const m=String(text||"").match(/^\(([^)]+)\)/);
  const token=(m?.[1]||"").trim();
  if(!token) return ["",""];
  if(/부$|부\s/.test(token)) return [token.replace(/\s*확정$/,""),""];
  const parts=token.split(/\s+/);
  if(parts.length>1 && /부$/.test(parts[0])) return [parts[0],parts.slice(1).join(" ")];
  return ["",token.replace(/\s*확정$/," ").trim()];
}
async function sheetsRequest(token,url,options={}) {
  const response=await fetch(url,{...options,headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"content-type":"application/json",...(options.headers||{})}});
  if(!response.ok){let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""}catch{};throw new Error(`Google Sheets 쓰기 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`)}
  return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
}
async function replaceCalendarRows(token, rows=[]) {
  const base=`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID}/values`;
  await sheetsRequest(token,`${base}/${encodeURIComponent("'41_학교캘린더'!A4:W3000")}:clear`,{method:"POST",body:"{}"});
  if(rows.length) await sheetsRequest(token,`${base}/${encodeURIComponent(`'41_학교캘린더'!A4:W${rows.length+3}`)}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`,{method:"PUT",body:JSON.stringify({majorDimension:"ROWS",values:rows})});
}
async function ensureCalendarSchema(token) {
  const base=`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID}/values`;
  const values=[["41_학교캘린더 · UEP 직접이관 DB"],["기본정보 연결시트나 IMPORTRANGE를 사용하지 않고 직접 기록된 일정만 보관합니다."],["일정ID","학년도","시작일","종료일","시작시각","종료시각","종일","일정명","상세내용","일정구분","대상","장소","부서","담당자","상태","검증상태","출처","원본시작셀","원본끝셀","원본일정ID","이관방식","수정일시","비고"]];
  await sheetsRequest(token,`${base}/${encodeURIComponent("'41_학교캘린더'!A1:W3")}?valueInputOption=USER_ENTERED`,{method:"PUT",body:JSON.stringify({majorDimension:"ROWS",values})});
}
async function currentCalendarRows(token) {
  const vr=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'41_학교캘린더'!A1:W3000"]);
  const values=vr?.[0]?.values||[]; const header=(values[0]||[]).map(String);
  return values.slice(1).filter(r=>r.some(v=>String(v||"").trim())).map(r=>Object.fromEntries(header.map((h,i)=>[h,r[i]??""])));
}
function calendarObjectToRow(row){
  const start=String(row["시작일"]||row["일자"]||"");
  return [row["일정ID"],row["학년도"]||start.slice(0,4),start,row["종료일"]||row["종료일자"]||start,row["시작시각"],row["종료시각"],row["종일"]||(!row["시작시각"]?"Y":"N"),row["일정명"]||row["프로그램명"],row["상세내용"]||row["비고"],row["일정구분"]||"업무",row["대상"],row["장소"],row["부서"],row["담당자"],row["상태"]||"확정",row["검증상태"]||"정상",row["출처"]||"UEP 직접입력",row["원본시작셀"],row["원본끝셀"],row["원본일정ID"],row["이관방식"]||"직접기록",row["수정일시"],row["비고"]];
}


function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.

 {
    try {
      const recovered = await readCredentialRecovery();
      if (validateServiceAccount(recovered)) {
        try { if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(recovered))); } catch {}
        return recovered;
      }
    } catch {}
  }
  const backup = `${filePath}.unreadable-${Date.now()}.bak`;
  try { await fs.rename(filePath, backup); } catch {}
  const error = new Error("저장된 보안 인증정보를 복호화할 수 없습니다. 서비스 계정 JSON을 다시 등록해 주세요.");
  error.code = "UEP_SAFE_STORAGE_DECRYPT_FAILED";
  throw error;
}

let schoolReadPolicyCache={url:'',expiresAt:0};
function schoolReadError(message,code='UEP_SCHOOL_READ_ERROR'){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadSchoolReadApiUrl(){
  if(schoolReadPolicyCache.url&&schoolReadPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now())return schoolReadPolicyCache.url;
  let response;
  try{response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:'no-store'});}catch(error){throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE');}
  if(!response.ok)throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 설정 조회 실패 ('+response.status+')','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE');
  const policy=await response.json();
  const url=String(policy?.schoolReadApiUrl||UEP_SCHOOL_READ_API_FALLBACK||'').trim();
  if(!/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/s\/[^/]+\/exec(?:\?.*)?$/i.test(url))throw schoolReadError('UEP School Read API 배포 URL이 아직 등록되지 않았습니다.','UEP_SCHOOL_READ_NOT_CONFIGURED');
  schoolReadPolicyCache={url,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return url;
}
async function getOrCreateSchoolReadDeviceId(){
  try{const saved=String(await fs.readFile(schoolReadDevicePath(),'utf8')).trim();if(saved)return saved;}catch{}
  const id='UEP-'+crypto.randomUUID();
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(schoolReadDevicePath()),{recursive:true});
  await fs.writeFile(schoolReadDevicePath(),id,'utf8');
  return id;
}

  try{const value=await readEncrypted(schoolReadSessionPath());return value&&typeof value==='object'?value:null;}catch{return null;}

async function schoolReadPost(action,payload={}){
  const url=await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();
  const response=await net.fetch(url,{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/json;charset=UTF-8','accept':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({action,...payload})});
  let data={};try{data=await response.json();}catch{}
  if(!response.ok)throw schoolReadError(data?.message||('School Read API HTTP '+response.status),data?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_HTTP_ERROR');
  return data;
}

  return result;
}

async function schoolReadSessionStatus({verify=true}={}){
  let endpointReady=true,endpointError='';
  try{await loadSchoolReadApiUrl();}catch(error){endpointReady=false;endpointError=String(error?.message||error);}
  const session=await readSchoolReadSession();
  if(!session?.token||!session?.deviceId)return {ok:endpointReady,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:endpointError,user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  if(!verify)return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady,user:session.user||null,mode:'school_read_api'};
  try{const result=await schoolReadPost('verify-token',{token:session.token,deviceId:session.deviceId});if(!result?.ok)throw schoolReadError(result?.message||'UEP 세션 확인 실패',result?.code||'UEP_SCHOOL_READ_TOKEN_INVALID');session.user=result.user||session.user||{};await saveSchoolReadSession(session);return {ok:true,authenticated:true,endpointReady:true,user:session.user,expiresAt:result.expiresAt||'',mode:'school_read_api'};}catch(error){await clearSchoolReadSession();return {ok:false,authenticated:false,endpointReady,reason:String(error?.message||error),code:error?.code||'',user:null,mode:'school_read_api'};}
}

  return result.valueRanges||[];
}

// __UEP_GOOGLE_USER_OAUTH_08070__
const UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/hkhsm00-k81c/UEP-Updates/main/uep-policy.json";
let googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:"",expiresAt:0};

function googleOAuthError(message,code){const e=new Error(message);e.code=code;return e;}
async function loadGoogleOAuthClientId(){
  if(googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId && googleOAuthPolicyCache.expiresAt>Date.now()) return googleOAuthPolicyCache.clientId;
  let response;
  try{ response=await net.fetch(UEP_UPDATE_POLICY_URL,{cache:"no-store"}); }
  catch(error){ throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결 설정을 확인하지 못했습니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE"); }
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 연결 설정 조회 실패 (${response.status})`,"UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_POLICY_UNAVAILABLE");
  const policy=await response.json();
  const clientId=String(policy?.googleOAuthClientId||"").trim();
  if(!/\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$/i.test(clientId)) throw googleOAuthError("관리자가 UEP Google OAuth 클라이언트를 아직 설정하지 않았습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_NOT_CONFIGURED");
  googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId,expiresAt:Date.now()+10*60*1000};
  return clientId;
}
function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
  const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);
}
function encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
  const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
  const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);
  return {version:1,algorithm:'aes-256-gcm',salt:salt.toString('base64'),iv:iv.toString('base64'),tag:cipher.getAuthTag().toString('base64'),data:encrypted.toString('base64')};
}
function decodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(payload){
  if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=='aes-256-gcm')throw new Error('invalid OAuth recovery token');
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,'base64'),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,'base64'),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,'base64'),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,'base64');
  const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv('aes-256-gcm',googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString('utf8'));
}
async function writeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
  const payload=encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value);
  for(const target of [googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath()]){
    await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(target),{recursive:true});
    await fs.writeFile(target,JSON.stringify(payload),'utf8');
  }
}
async function readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(){
  for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{return decodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(target,'utf8')));}catch{}
  }
  throw new Error('OAuth recovery token not found');
}

async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
  for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
    try{
      const encrypted=await fs.readFile(target);
      if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
        try{const value=JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));if(value&&typeof value==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(value);return value;}}catch{}
      }
      try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
    }catch{}
  }
  try{
    const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
    if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(recovered);return recovered;}
  }catch{}
  return null;
}
async function googleUserInfo(accessToken){
  const response=await net.fetch('https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo',{headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${accessToken}`}});
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 사용자 확인 실패 (${response.status})`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_USERINFO_FAILED');
  return await response.json();
}
async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}
async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

async function authorizeGoogleUser({loginHint=''}={}){
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

  let n=Number(index)+1, out="";
  while(n>0){ const r=(n-1)%26; out=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+out; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26); }
  return out;
}

async function clearSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range) {
  const url = `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:clear`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: "{}",
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공결 삭제 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
  const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
  const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
  const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
  const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
    String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
    String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
    String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
    String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
    String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),
    unit, scope, String(payload.originalType||"공결"), String(payload.sourceTab||"UEP 직접등록"), String(payload.sourceRow||"")
  ]];
}

  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];

  let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
  let sheetRow=-1;
  if(id){
    const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
    if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
  }else{
    let max=0;
    rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
    id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
  }
  const values=officialAttendanceRow(payload,id);
  if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
  const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
  return {ok:true,id,patch:operational.patch||{}};
}

async function ocrInputCenterBatch(payload={}){
  const files=Array.isArray(payload.files)?payload.files:[];const results=await mapWithConcurrency(files,2,async f=>({key:String(f.key||''),path:String(f.path||''),...(await ocrInputCenterFile(String(f.path||'')))}));return {ok:true,count:results.length,results};

async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
  const queue = Array.from(items || []);
  const results = new Array(queue.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
    while (true) {
      const index = cursor++;
      if (index >= queue.length) return;
      results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
    }
  });
  await Promise.all(runners);
  return results;
}

// v0.80.40: report normalization helpers must be module-scoped because fetchLiveData uses them during Google Sheet refresh.
function inputCenterReportPairs(meta={}){
  const fields=Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[];
  const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
  return fields.map((f,index)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${index+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
}
function inputCenterReportText(meta={}){
  return inputCenterReportPairs(meta).map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
}

function normalizeSelectedReportProgramName(value=""){

}

function ymd(value = new Date()) {
  const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);
  return `${date.getFullYear()}${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function mondayOf(dateValue) {
  const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
  date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
  const day = date.getDay();
  date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
  return date;
}

async function fetchJson(url, timeoutMs = 12000) {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);
  try {
    const response = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: { "user-agent": "UEP/0.42.7 School Operations" },
    });
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공공데이터 연결 실패 (${response.status})`);
    return await response.json();
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

function neisRows(payload, key) {
  const group = payload?.[key];
  if (!Array.isArray(group)) return [];
  const rowBlock = group.find((item) => Array.isArray(item?.row));
  return rowBlock?.row || [];
}

function neisResultMessage(payload, key) {
  const result = payload?.RESULT || payload?.[key]?.[0]?.head?.find?.((item) => item.RESULT)?.RESULT;
  if (!result) return "";
  return result.MESSAGE || result.CODE || "";
}

function neisUrl(dataset, params, apiKey = "") {
  const url = new URL(`https://open.neis.go.kr/hub/${dataset}`);
  url.searchParams.set("Type", "json");
  url.searchParams.set("pIndex", "1");
  url.searchParams.set("pSize", "100");
  if (apiKey) url.searchParams.set("KEY", apiKey);
  Object.entries(params || {}).forEach(([key, value]) => {
    if (value !== "" && value !== null && value !== undefined) url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
  });
  return url.toString();
}

async function findNeisSchool({ schoolName, officeCode = "", schoolCode = "", apiKey = "" }) {
  if (officeCode && schoolCode) return { schoolName, officeCode, schoolCode };
  const payload = await fetchJson(neisUrl("schoolInfo", { SCHUL_NM: schoolName }, apiKey));
  const rows = neisRows(payload, "schoolInfo");
  const exact = rows.find((row) => String(row.SCHUL_NM || "").replace(/\s/g, "") === String(schoolName || "").replace(/\s/g, ""));
  const row = exact || rows[0];
  if (!row) throw new Error(neisResultMessage(payload, "schoolInfo") || `${schoolName} 학교정보를 찾지 못했습니다.`);
  return {
    schoolName: row.SCHUL_NM || schoolName,
    officeCode: row.ATPT_OFCDC_SC_CODE,
    schoolCode: row.SD_SCHUL_CODE,
    officeName: row.ATPT_OFCDC_SC_NM || "",
    address: row.ORG_RDNMA || row.ORG_RDNZC || "",
  };
}

function cleanMealText(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .replace(/<br\s*\/?\s*>/gi, "\n")
    .replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, (match) => match)
    .split(/\n+/)
    .map((item) => item.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
