}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

async function clearSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range) {
  const url = `https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:clear`;
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: "{}",
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공결 삭제 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
  const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
  const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
  const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
  const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
    String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
    String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
    String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
    String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
    String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),
    unit, scope, String(payload.originalType||"공결"), String(payload.sourceTab||"UEP 직접등록"), String(payload.sourceRow||"")
  ]];
}

async function saveOfficialAttendance(payload={}) {
  const account=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
  if(!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("저장된 Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 올바르지 않습니다.");
  const token=await getSheetsToken(account);
  const range="'30_공결기록'!A3:Z3000";
  const result=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,[range]);
  const matrix=result?.[0]?.values||[];
  const rows=matrix.slice(1);
  let id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
  let sheetRow=-1;
  if(id){
    const pos=rows.findIndex(row=>String(row?.[0]||"").trim()===id);
    if(pos>=0) sheetRow=pos+4;
  }else{
    let max=0;
    rows.forEach(row=>{const m=String(row?.[0]||"").match(/^ATT-2026-(\\d+)$/);if(m)max=Math.max(max,Number(m[1]));});
    id=`ATT-2026-${String(max+1).padStart(4,"0")}`;
  }
  const values=officialAttendanceRow(payload,id);
  if(sheetRow>0) await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'30_공결기록'!A${sheetRow}:Z${sheetRow}`,values);
  else await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,"'30_공결기록'!A:Z",values);
  const operational=await fetchOperationalData();
  return {ok:true,id,patch:operational.patch||{}};
}

async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
  const queue = Array.from(items || []);
  const results = new Array(queue.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
    while (true) {
      const index = cursor++;
      if (index >= queue.length) return;
      results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
    }
  });
  await Promise.all(runners);
  return results;
}

// v0.80.40: report normalization helpers must be module-scoped because fetchLiveData uses them during Google Sheet refresh.
function inputCenterReportPairs(meta={}){
  const fields=Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[];
  const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
  return fields.map((f,index)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${index+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
}
function inputCenterReportText(meta={}){
  return inputCenterReportPairs(meta).map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
}

function normalizeSelectedReportProgramName(value=""){
  return String(value||"").normalize("NFKC").trim().replace(/\s*\((?:\d{4}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}\.?)?(?:\s*[~～\-–—]\s*\d{4}[./-]\d{1,2}[./-]\d{1,2}\.?)?\)\s*$/g,"").replace(/\s+/g," ").trim();
}
function reportDateTimeText(value=""){
  if(value instanceof Date&&!Number.isNaN(value.getTime()))return `${value.getFullYear()}-${String(value.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(value.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(value.getHours()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(value.getSeconds()).padStart(2,'0')}`;
  const raw=String(value||"").trim();
  const kr=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})[.\/-]\s*(\d{1,2})\.?\s*(오전|오후)?\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
  if(kr){let hour=Number(kr[5]);if(kr[4]==='오후'&&hour<12)hour+=12;if(kr[4]==='오전'&&hour===12)hour=0;return `${kr[1]}-${String(kr[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(kr[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(hour).padStart(2,'0')}:${kr[6]}:${kr[7]||'00'}`;}
  const m=raw.match(/^(\d{4})[./-](\d{1,2})[./-](\d{1,2})(?:\s+|T)(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?/);
  return m?`${m[1]}-${String(m[2]).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(m[3]).padStart(2,'0')} ${String(m[4]).padStart(2,'0')}:${m[5]}:${m[6]||'00'}`:raw;
}

unction classifyCalendarStatus(text) {
  const value=String(text||"");
  if(/취소|폐지/.test(value)) return "취소";
  if(/확정/.test(value)) return "확정";
  if(/예약|대관|사용/.test(value)) return "예약";
  if(/예정|계획|공고|제출기간/.test(value)) return "예정";
  return "상태 미기재";
}
function parseOwner(text) {
  const m=String(text||"").match(/^\(([^)]+)\)/);
  const token=(m?.[1]||"").trim();
  if(!token) return ["",""];
  if(/부$|부\s/.test(token)) return [token.replace(/\s*확정$/,""),""];
  const parts=token.split(/\s+/);
  if(parts.length>1 && /부$/.test(parts[0])) return [parts[0],parts.slice(1).join(" ")];
  return ["",token.replace(/\s*확정$/," ").trim()];
}
async function sheetsRequest(token,url,options={}) {
  const response=await fetch(url,{...options,headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"content-type":"application/json",...(options.headers||{})}});
  if(!response.ok){let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""}catch{};throw new Error(`Google Sheets 쓰기 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`)}
  return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
}

}

function calendarObjectToRow(row){
  const start=String(row["시작일"]||row["일자"]||"");
  return [row["일정ID"],row["학년도"]||start.slice(0,4),start,row["종료일"]||row["종료일자"]||start,row["시작시각"],row["종료시각"],row["종일"]||(!row["시작시각"]?"Y":"N"),row["일정명"]||row["프로그램명"],row["상세내용"]||row["비고"],row["일정구분"]||"업무",row["대상"],row["장소"],row["부서"],row["담당자"],row["상태"]||"확정",row["검증상태"]||"정상",row["출처"]||"UEP 직접입력",row["원본시작셀"],row["원본끝셀"],row["원본일정ID"],row["이관방식"]||"직접기록",row["수정일시"],row["비고"]];
}


function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);

  return response.status===204?{}:response.json();
}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);

      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

function assignValueRangesByRange(target, entries, valueRanges) {
  const logicalBySheet=new Map(entries.map(([logicalName,range])=>[sheetNameFromA1Range(range),logicalName]));
  const assigned=new Set();
  (Array.isArray(valueRanges)?valueRanges:[]).forEach((item,index)=>{
    const responseSheet=sheetNameFromA1Range(item?.range);
    // Sheets batchGet may omit empty ranges or return them in a different order.
    // A response that names its range must only be assigned to that sheet; index is
    // retained solely for legacy responses that contain no range metadata at all.
    const logicalName=responseSheet?logicalBySheet.get(responseSheet):entries[index]?.[0];
    if(!logicalName)return;
    target[logicalName]=item?.values||[];
    assigned.add(logicalName);
  });
  entries.forEach(([logicalName])=>{if(!assigned.has(logicalName)&&!target[logicalName])target[logicalName]=[];});
  return target;
}

async function updateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values, attempt=0) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "PUT",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail=""; try{ detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||""; }catch{}
    if ((response.status===429 || response.status>=500) && attempt<4) {
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return updateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,range,values,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
  const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
  if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
  const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
  const response=await fetch(url,{
    method:"POST",
    headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${token}`,"Content-Type":"application/json"},
    body:JSON.stringify({valueInputOption:"USER_ENTERED",data:items.map(x=>({range:x.range,majorDimension:"ROWS",values:x.values}))}),
  });
  if(!response.ok){
    let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
      const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
      const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
      return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    }
    throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
  }
  return response.json();
}

function officialAttendanceRow(payload={}, id="") {
  const now=new Date().toISOString();
  const type=String(payload.detailType||"결석");
  const start=String(payload.startPeriod||"");
  const end=String(payload.endPeriod||"");
  const unit=(start||end)?"교시":"일일";
  const scope=(start||end)?(start&&end?`${start}~${end}교시`:`${start||end}교시`):"하루 전체";
  return [[
    id,
    String(payload.studentId||""), String(payload.studentNo||""), String(payload.name||""),
    String(payload.grade||"1"), String(payload.className||payload.classNo||""), String(payload.date||""),
    String(payload.attendanceType||"출석인정"), type, start, end,
    String(payload.reasonText||payload.reason||""), String(payload.place||""),
    String(payload.evidenceRequired||"N"), String(payload.evidence||"불필요"),
    String(payload.signatureRequired||"N"), String(payload.signatureStatus||"불필요"),
    String(payload.registrar||"UEP 직접등록"), now, String(payload.editor||"UEP"), String(payload.note||""),
    unit, scope, String(payload.originalType||"공결"), String(payload.sourceTab||"UEP 직접등록"), String(payload.sourceRow||"")
  ]];
}

async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
  url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
  url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
  return response.json();
}

async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
  const queue = Array.from(items || []);
  const results = new Array(queue.length);
  let cursor = 0;
  const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
    while (true) {
      const index = cursor++;
      if (index >= queue.length) return;
      results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
    }
  });
  await Promise.all(runners);
  return results;
}

function inputCenterReportPairs(meta={}){
  const fields=Array.isArray(meta.fields)?meta.fields:[];
  const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
  return fields.map((f,index)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${index+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
}
function inputCenterReportText(meta={}){
  return inputCenterReportPairs(meta).map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
}

}

async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
  for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){
    try{await fs.unlink(file);}catch(error){if(error?.code!=='ENOENT')throw error;}
  }
  liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;return {ok:true};
}
async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
  const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
    iss: credentials.client_email,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
    aud: credentials.token_uri,
    iat: now,
    exp: now + 3600,
  }));
  const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
  const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
  const body = new URLSearchParams({
    grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
    assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
  });
  const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
    body,
  });
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
  const payload = await response.json();
  if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
  return payload.access_token;
}

async function readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt = 0) {
  if(String(token||'').startsWith('UEP_SCHOOL_READ:')) return schoolReadBatchRead(spreadsheetId,ranges);
  const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchGet`);
  url.searchParams.set("majorDimension", "ROWS");
  url.searchParams.set("valueRenderOption", "UNFORMATTED_VALUE");
  url.searchParams.set("dateTimeRenderOption", "SERIAL_NUMBER");
  ranges.forEach((range) => url.searchParams.append("ranges", range));
  const response = await fetch(url, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = "";
    try { detail = (await response.json())?.error?.message || ""; } catch {}
    if ((response.status === 429 || response.status >= 500) && attempt < 3) {
      const retryAfter = Number(response.headers.get("retry-after") || 0);
      const waitMs = retryAfter > 0 ? retryAfter * 1000 : 900 * Math.pow(2, attempt);
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, waitMs));
      return readSheetBatch(token, spreadsheetId, ranges, attempt + 1);
    }
    throw new Error(`UEP 구글시트 읽기 실패 (${response.status})${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()).valueRanges || [];
}

function sheetNameFromA1Range(range="") {
  const prefix=String(range||"").split("!")[0].trim();
  return prefix.replace(/^'/,"").replace(/'$/,"").replace(/''/g,"'");
}

async function currentCalendarRows(token) {
  const vr=await readSheetBatch(token,UEP_PROCESSING_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'41_학교캘린더'!A1:W3000"]);
  const values=vr?.[0]?.values||[]; const header=(values[0]||[]).map(String);
  return values.slice(1).filter(r=>r.some(v=>String(v||"").trim())).map(r=>Object.fromEntries(header.map((h,i)=>[h,r[i]??""])));
}
function calendarObjectToRow(row){
  const start=String(row["시작일"]||row["일자"]||"");
  return [row["일정ID"],row["학년도"]||start.slice(0,4),start,row["종료일"]||row["종료일자"]||start,row["시작시각"],row["종료시각"],row["종일"]||(!row["시작시각"]?"Y":"N"),row["일정명"]||row["프로그램명"],row["상세내용"]||row["비고"],row["일정구분"]||"업무",row["대상"],row["장소"],row["부서"],row["담당자"],row["상태"]||"확정",row["검증상태"]||"정상",row["출처"]||"UEP 직접입력",row["원본시작셀"],row["원본끝셀"],row["원본일정ID"],row["이관방식"]||"직접기록",row["수정일시"],row["비고"]];
}


function validateServiceAccount(value) {
  return Boolean(
    value && value.type === "service_account" &&
    typeof value.client_email === "string" &&
    typeof value.private_key === "string" &&
    value.private_key.startsWith("-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----") &&
    value.token_uri === "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token"
  );
}

function recoveryKey(salt) {
  // safeStorage가 Windows/Chromium 키 문제로 복호화에 실패할 때만 사용하는 로컬 복구키입니다.
  // 동일 Windows 사용자/PC에서 재부팅 후에도 안정적으로 재생성됩니다.
  const identity = `${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-CREDENTIAL-RECOVERY-v1`;
  return crypto.scryptSync(identity, salt, 32);
}

async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
  const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
  const key = recoveryKey(salt);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
  const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const payload = {
    version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
    salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
    tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
  };
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
  await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
}

async function readCredentialRecovery() {
  const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
  if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
  const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
  decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
  return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
}

async function saveEncrypted(filePath, value) {
  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  if (safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()) {
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, safeStorage.encryptString(JSON.stringify(value)));
  } else {
    // 인증정보는 아래 복구 저장소가 있으므로, 다른 보안값은 기존처럼 실패시킵니다.
    if (path.resolve(filePath) !== path.resolve(credentialPath())) throw new Error("Windows 보안 저장소를 사용할 수 없습니다.");
  }
  if (path.resolve(filePath) === path.resolve(credentialPath())) await writeCredentialRecovery(value);
}

async function googleUserInfo(accessToken){
  const response=await net.fetch('https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo',{headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${accessToken}`}});
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 사용자 확인 실패 (${response.status})`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_USERINFO_FAILED');
  return await response.json();
}
async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}
async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}

}

async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
  for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
    try{
      const encrypted=await fs.readFile(target);
      if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
        try{const value=JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));if(value&&typeof value==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(value);return value;}}catch{}
      }
      try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
    }catch{}
  }
  try{
    const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
    if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(recovered);return recovered;}
  }catch{}
  return null;
}
async function googleUserInfo(accessToken){
  const response=await net.fetch('https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo',{headers:{authorization:`Bearer ${accessToken}`}});
  if(!response.ok) throw googleOAuthError(`Google 사용자 확인 실패 (${response.status})`,'UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_USERINFO_FAILED');
  return await response.json();
}
async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
  if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  const clientId=await loadGoogleOAuthClientId();
  if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
    await disconnectGoogleUser();
    throw googleOAuthError('Google 연결 설정이 갱신되었습니다. Google 계정 연결을 한 번만 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
  }
  const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
  const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
  if(!response.ok){
    let payload={},detail='',errCode='';
    try{payload=await response.json();detail=String(payload?.error_description||'');errCode=String(payload?.error||'');}catch{}
    if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){
      googleOAuthPolicyCache={clientId:'',expiresAt:0};
      await disconnectGoogleUser();
      throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
    }
    throw googleOAuthError(`Google 계정 토큰 갱신 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:''}`,"UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  }
  const next=await response.json();
  const merged={...saved,...next,client_id:clientId,expires_at:Date.now()+Math.max(60,Number(next.expires_in||3600))*1000,updated_at:new Date().toISOString()};
  await saveGoogleUserOAuth(merged);
  return merged;
}
async function getGoogleUserSheetsToken(){
  let saved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
  if(!saved) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결이 필요합니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  if(saved.access_token && Number(saved.expires_at||0)>Date.now()+90000) return String(saved.access_token);
  saved=await refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved);
  if(!saved.access_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 액세스 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");
  return String(saved.access_token);
}
